1736 喬山(Johnson Health)

報告日期： 2026/06/02 研 究 員：洪  國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 公告 1Q26 稅後 EPS 0.08 元 
喬山公告 1Q26 營收金額 117.92 億元， YoY+11.15% ，
QoQ-39.13% ，營業利益 -0.93 億元， YoY-206.5% ，
QoQ-104.89% ， 稅後盈餘0.24 億元 ，YoY-91.34% ，QoQ-98.6% ，
稅後 EPS 0.08 元。

二. 預估 2Q26 稅後 EPS 1.98 元 
喬山 04/2026 營收金額 39.98 億元， YoY+6.4% ，MoM-6.31% 。
預估喬山 2Q26 營收金額 133.03 億元， YoY+8.1% ，
QoQ+12.81% ， 預估營業利益10.96 億元 ，YoY+31.29% ，較 1Q26
由虧轉盈，預估稅後盈餘 6.00 億元， YoY+436.25% ，
QoQ+2409% ，預估稅後 EPS 1.98 元。

三 . 預估 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元，
YoY+15.98%  
商用市場 1Q26 營收金額 2.35 億美元， YoY+17.21% 。商用市場
2026 年營運成長動能包括 ：1， 在歐美健身房主要客戶滲透率持續
提升，另外也拓展歐美各國本土前五大健身房客戶。 2，產品線擴
展，增加高階健身器材產品線，以便拓展高端健身房市場，增加新
客群。 3，商用健身器材零組件主要以台灣與中國上海為主，預期
在越南工廠生產比重提升帶動下，將有助整體毛利率提升。 預估喬
山 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元， YoY+15.98% 。

四. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 13.00 元 
喬山 2026 年在歐美健身房客戶持續在海外設據點 ， 加上客戶轉單
效益 ， 搭配旗下越南廠生產效率提升與產能擴充 ， 將有助喬山2026
年營運持續成長。宏遠預估喬山 2026 年營收金額 630.00 億元，
YoY+15.96% ，預估營業利益 59.86 億元， YoY+66.66% ，預估稅
後盈餘 39.44 億元， YoY+43.43% ，預估稅後 EPS 13.00 元。

五. 投資建議買進，目標價 156 元 
觀察喬山歷史 PER 落在 8~33X，2026 年 ROE 26.7%，PER 9.1X，
評價在低檔水準，加上 2026 年營運成長，故投資建議買進，目標
價 156 元。 (12X 2026 年稅後 EPS) 
投資評等：買進 
目標價位：156.0 元 
     12.0X 2026 EPS 
參考價位：118.5 元 
     9.1X 2026 EPS 
     2026/06/01 收盤

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
喬山健康科技創立於 1975 年，資本額 30.34 億元，並於 2003 年
掛牌上市，喬山為亞洲第一、世界前三大的國際專業運動健身器
材集團公司，產品包括電動跑步機、室內健身車、橢圓訓練機、
重量訓練器材、划船器材、電動按摩椅、心肺訓練器材等。喬山
以「健康、價值、共享」為企業經營理念，專注於健康科技事業
的發展，並以 Matrix 、Vision 和 Horizon 自有品牌行銷全世界 80
餘國。生產基地位於台中、上海、與美國越南，其中台中生產高
單價的商用產品、上海主要生產製造量大的家用產品與小部分商
用產品、美國主要生產製造特殊重量訓練產品、越南主要生產製
造家用產品為主。

二.品牌定位與銷售通路  
喬山旗下擁有 Matrix 、Vision 和 Horizon 三項自有品牌，其中
Martrix 主打高價位商用市場 ， 其主要銷售對象包括健身房 、 飯店、
運動員訓練中心、大型公司、運動專賣店等； Vision 主打中高價
位的市場，主要銷售對象以大型公司、運動專賣店為主； Horizon
主打家用市場為主，銷售對象包括運動專賣店、電商與大型運動
器材專賣店。

圖四、喬山旗下品牌定位與通路

資料來源：喬山 &宏遠投顧彙整

三. 2026 年營運概況及展望

全球健身器材市場 100 億美元 (宏遠投顧推估 )，其中商用市場 50
億美元、家用市場 50 億美元。觀察全球主要廠商 2024 年營收金
額概況： Life 6.75 億美元， YoY+4.2%( 宏遠投顧推估 )；TG 7.67
億美元 ，YoY+13.6%；喬 山10.39 億美元 ，YoY+18.3%；Precor2.61
億美元， YoY+26.1% ；Pelonton 27.0 億美元， YoY-1.1% 。

預估 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元， YoY+15.98%

喬山商用市場客戶包括 ： 健身房北美星球健身 、 歐洲BASIC-FIT 、
拉丁美洲 Smart Fit 以及全球連鎖性健身房 Anytime Finess 、台 灣
World GYM 與健身工廠等；飯店客戶 Marriott 、STARWOOD 、
FOUR SEASONS 等。

圖五、喬山健身房客戶

資料來源：喬山 &宏遠投顧彙整

商用市場 2025 年營收金額 12.33 億美元， YoY+18.67% 。觀察喬
山商用市場 2025 年營運成長動能包括： 1，在中國大陸健身器材
出口至美國課徵高額關稅 ， 相對於台灣與越南關稅較低，帶動健身
房客戶訂單轉向台灣與越南地區。 2，歐洲大型客戶持續拓展美國
市場，另外美國主要客戶也拓展歐洲市場，歐洲與美國主要客戶
2025 年分別成長 25~28% 與 21~24% 。3，歐洲地區受惠中小型客
戶需求增溫，帶動歐洲地成長 25~30% 。

商用市場 1Q26 營收金額 2.35 億美元， YoY+17.21% 。商用市場
2026 年營運成長動能包括 ：1， 在歐美健身房主要客戶滲透率持續
提升，另外也拓展歐美各國本土前五大健身房客戶。 2，產品線擴
展，增加高階健身器材產品線，以便拓展高端健身房市場，增加新
客群。 3，商用健身器材零組件主要以台灣與中國上海為主，預期
在越南工廠生產比重提升帶動下，將有助整體毛利率提升。 預估喬
山 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元， YoY+15.98% 。

圖六、擴展商用市場新產品 /新通路

資料來源：喬山 &宏遠投顧彙整

預估 2026 年家用市場營收金額 4.00 億美元， YoY+12.68%

喬山家用市場主要以運動專賣店、電商與大型運動器材專賣店為
主。喬山為拓展家用市場零售通路，陸續在 2015 年~2016 年購併
2nd Wind 、BUSY BODY 、Leisure Fitness 與 Gym Source 等健
身器材銷售通路，喬山在北美銷售通路據點共 103 家，據點數占
北美 30% 。

家用市場 2025 年營收金額 3.55 億美元 ，YoY+17.16% 。喬 山2025
年家用營運成長主要包括： 1，BowFlex 在 2025 年貢獻營收金額
1.1~1.2 億美元， YoY+53~67% 。2，觀察歐洲地區與拉丁美洲受
惠家用健身器材營運基期較低，商用健身房興起帶動家用市場需
求熱絡，帶動歐洲地區與拉丁美洲家用市場分別成長 45~48% 與
22~25% 。3，觀察全球主要家用健身器材廠商，生產基地集中在
中國大陸，在美國對中國大陸出口產品課徵高額關稅因素，許多
訂單己移至越南生產，帶動喬山越南廠稼動率提升。

6 
家用市場 1Q26 營收金額 1.01 億美元， YoY+12.85% 。家用市場
2026 年營運成長動能包括： 1，家用健身器材經歷 2020~2021 年
上半年疫情期間大好， 2021 下半年至 2023 年庫存調整， 2025 年
因關稅訂單偏保守，觀察零售通路庫存已達相對低檔水準，加上
己有廠商退出市場，將有助家用市場成長。 2，喬山持續拓展零售
據點效益顯現，例如大型量販店己向喬山採購多項家用產品線，
帶動大型量販通路營收。 3，家用旗下 BowFlex 在 2026 年推出新
產品，搶攻北美家用市場，將有助 BowFlex  2026 年出貨成長。
綜合上述，預估 2025 年喬山家用市場營收金額 4.00 億美元，
YoY+12.68% 。

預估富士醫療 (含自有品牌按摩椅 )貢獻 2026 年營收金額 1.70 億
美元， YoY+6.9%

喬山 10/29/2019 公告，以 67 億日幣 （約當台幣20 億元），向「朝
日生活與健康股份有限公司 Asahi Life & Health Corporation 」買
下子公司「富士醫療器股份有限公司 FUJI MEDICAL 
INSTRUMENT 」 60% 股權，「富士醫療器股份有限公司 FUJI 
MEDICAL INSTRUMENT 」為日本最大按摩椅製造商，擁有業界
領先產品開發和品牌實力，另外在日本佈有綿密的銷售通路，觀
察富士醫療器年營收金額 180~200 億日幣 (約當美元 1.8~2 億美
元)，己於 03/2020 完成股權交割。喬山 12/09/2022 公告取得富
士醫療器 40% 股權 ， 交易完成後喬山持有富士醫療器100% 股權。

圖七、喬山建立全球品牌，邁向國際

富士醫療 2025 年營收金額 1.28 億美元， YoY+9.4% 。自有品牌按
摩椅 2025 年營收金額 0.31 億美元， YoY+6.9% 。觀察富士醫療營
收成長主因在於富士醫療拓展日本以外的外銷市場有成。

富士醫療 + 自有品牌按摩椅 1Q26 營收金額 0.36 億美元，
YoY+9.09% 預期 2026 年，在富士醫療持續拓展日本以外的外銷
市場，加上喬山自有品牌按摩椅穩定成長。預估富士醫療 2026 年
營收金額 1.36 億美元， YoY+6.25% ；預估喬山自有品牌按摩椅
2026 年營收金額 0.34 億元 ，YoY+9.68%， 合計兩者貢獻喬山2026
年營收金額 1.70 億美元， YoY+6.9% 。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

預估 2026 年稅後盈餘 39.44 億元 ，YoY+43.43% ，稅 後EPS 13.00
元

喬山 2026 年在歐美健身房客戶持續在海外設據點 ， 加上客戶轉單
效益 ， 搭配旗下越南廠生產效率提升與產能擴充 ， 將有助喬山2026
年營運持續成長。宏遠預估喬山 2026 年營收金額 630.00 億元，
YoY+15.96% ，預估營業利益 59.86 億元， YoY+66.66% ，預估稅
後盈餘 39.44 億元， YoY+43.43% ，預估稅後 EPS 13.00 元。

公告配發現金股利 4.5 元，現金殖利率 3.8%

喬山歷年股利政策，主要以現金股利為主。回顧 2024 年，喬山股
利政策，配發現金股利 4 元，股利發放率 50% 。喬山公告 2025
年股利政策，將配發現金股利 4.5 元，現金殖利率 3.8% 。

## Document 056: 2308台達電 群益

Source PDF: all report pdf/2308台達電 群益.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-06-04
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 6 月 4 日

1 群益投顧 
台達電 (2308 TT) 於 COMPUTEX 2026 以「 Superior Efficiency, Shaping Sustainable 
AI 」 為 主 題 ， 提 出 多 項 創 新 解 決 方 案。 燃 料 電 池 與 固 態 變 壓 器 為 長 期 成 長 動 能 ，預期
2~3 年內將有較顯著營收貢獻 。台 達 電 為 AI 伺服器電源解決方案與水冷 Sidecar 主要
供 應 商，受 惠 於 AI、Data Center 領 域 需 求 成 長，營 收 與 獲 利 維 持 成 長 趨 勢，維 持Buy
投 資 評 等 ， 目 標 價 為 2,800 元 。

研 究 員 ： 王 美 珍   
iris.wang@capital.com.tw

燃 料 電 池 與 固 態 變 壓 器為 長 期 成 長 動 能：  
燃 料 電 池 具 備 高 效 率 、 可 堆 疊 特 性 。 公 司 已 規 劃 於 觀 音 廠 於2026 年 底 前 完
成 第 一 條 生 產 線 安 裝 ， 並 已 在 籌 備 觀 音 二 廠 擴 大 規 模 。 
在未來的 2~3 年 內 ， 包 含 燃 料 電 池 預 計 僅 佔 總 營 收約 1% ， 但 其 作 為 長 期 能
源 解 決 方 案 的 成 長 曲 線 非 常 明 確 。 
SST 目 前 已 少 量 出 貨 ， 客 戶 為 中 系 CSP 、 800V 高 壓 直 流 電 動 車 充 電 站 ， 亦
出 貨 給 美 系 客 戶 測 試 。SST 導 入 需 從 資 料 中 心 設 計 初 期 即 納 入 規 劃 ， 預 期 
2~3 年 後 會 產 生 顯 著 的 營 收 貢 獻 ， 仍 持 續 改 良 產 品 規 格 中 。 
雖然 SST 可 能 影 響 傳 統 AC/DC 電 源 業 務，但 SST 單 價 遠 高 於 電 源產品 ，應
是有利 。 台 達 電 在 AC/DC 電 源 業 務 市 佔 率極 高， 目前 亦 為 SST 進 度 最 快 的
廠商 ， 預 期 台 達 電 在 SST 仍 可 維 持 高市 佔 率 。  
台 達 電 為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 與 水 冷 Sidecar 主 要 供 應 商 ：  
為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商 。 受 惠 於 AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動 AC/DC
電 源 大 幅 成 長 外 ，CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC 電 源 模
組，亦 將 成 為 另 一 成 長 動 能。 NVIDIA 主 導 的 800 VDC 進 度 提 早 一 年，預 計
2026 年 生 產 並 出 貨 ；± 400VDC  2026 年 亦 會出貨。  
水冷散熱 以 Sidecar 為主 ， 2025 年、 1Q26 營收比重為 9% 。 台 達 電 Sidecar
產品 2025 年 市 佔 率 極 高， 雖 有 競 爭 者 加 入， 但 整 體 市 場 需 求 持 續 增 長 ， 故
2026 年 Sidecar 營 收 仍 將 維 持 成 長 趨 勢 。 
投 資 建 議 ：  
台達電 為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案與 水冷 Sidecar 主 要 供 應 商， 受惠於 AI 、
Data Center 領 域 需 求 成 長，營 收 與 獲 利 維 持 成 長 趨 勢，維持 Buy 投 資 評 等，
目標價為 2,800 元 。

相 關 個 股 獲 利 預 估 、 評 價 及 投 資 建 議

### Page 2

產業速報

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

報告日期：2026.06.04 研 究 員：李彝安

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 研究部將國巨目標價由 384 元調升至 1,020元（35x 28(F) PER），潛在上漲空間 24.4%。評
價基礎由 26F移至28F， 反映本輪被動元件漲價 、 稼動率回升與費用槓桿效益可望延續至2027
至 2028 年。投資評等維持「逢低買進」 。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/06/03 收盤價： 820.0 ➢ 國巨 26Q1 營運延續成長，26Q2 稼動率高於季初指
引。26Q1 營收 381.7 億元，QoQ +6.1%、YoY +22.7%，
毛利率 38.1%，EPS 3.90 元。26Q2 標準品與高階品
稼動率均高於季初法說指引，顯示需求回補已由鉭電容
延伸至 MLCC、電阻與電感等主要產品線。 
➢ Rubin平台升級推升 MLCC用量， 日韓產能排擠使國巨
受惠。VR200單機 MLCC美元含量與顆數均較 GB300
明顯增加，高容值 MLCC 佔比同步提升。日韓廠產能
優先配置高階 AI產品後，一般 IT 與中低階元件供給轉
緊。國巨在標準品、中高階 MLCC 及一般 IT 應用布局
完整，受惠重點在稼動率提升與報價環境改善。 
➢ 鉭電容漲價趨勢延續，提供非 MLCC 獲利支撐。國巨
透過 KEMET取得鉭質電容領先地位，AI 伺服器需求使
鉭電容訂單能見度延伸。鉭原料成本上行亦提供報價調
整基礎。 
➢ 預估 2026/27/28 年 EPS 為 17.38/23.80/29.18 元、
2026/27/28年營收 1,667.58（YoY +25.45%）、2,004.02
（YoY +20.18%）、2,382.22 億元（YoY +18.87%） 。受
惠 AI 需求帶動高階 MLCC/鉭電容供給趨緊、價格調
漲，以及產能排擠效應延續，研究部上修2026 至 2028
年獲利預估，並將預估此循環將延續至 2028F。考慮同
業 PER 評價區間及國巨產業定位，目標價 1,020 元
（35x 28(F) PER） ， 潛在上漲空間24.4%， 評等維持 「逢
低買進」 。

營 運 分 析 
國巨為全球被動元件龍頭，併購驅動一站式解決方案平台 
國巨股份有限公司成立於 1977 年，是全球領先的被動元件製造商，產品涵
蓋晶片電阻、積層陶瓷電容（MLCC）、鉭質電容、電感、電解電容、高頻
天線與感測器等，廣泛應用於車用電子、工業控制、通訊設備與 AI 伺服器
領域。目前國巨為全球最大晶片電阻與鉭質電容供應商，並居全球第三大  
MLCC 與電感製造商，擁有完整的產品組合與全球製造據點。 
近年來，國巨積極 透過海外併購與策略性投資擴大營運版圖，包含併購  
KEMET、Pulse、君耀及奇力新等廠商，跨足類比 IC 與功率元件領域。2025 
年 Q4 進一步完成收購日本 Shibaura（全球熱敏電阻市佔第一），結合先
前併入的 Nexensos（PTC 溫度感測器），在感測器領域形成完整產品線。 
Q1產品營收結構為磁性元件 25.2%、鉭質電容 24.3%、MLCC 19.0%、電
阻 13.5%、感測器 13.0%、其他 5.0%；25 年終端應用以工業 28.9% 佔
比最高，其次為電腦及企業系統 25.2%、車用 20.1%、消費電子 12.2%、
電信 10.3%、航太軍工醫療 3.4%。產品組合中高階品佔比約  75%、標準
品約 25%。區域別方面，美國 30.4%、亞洲 （不含中國）26.0%、中國 23.0%、
歐洲 20.5%；通路結構為經銷商 39.9%、OEM 37.8%、EMS 22.3%。 
圖一、國巨(2327) 2026Q1產品別營收比重。

資料來源：國巨，兆豐國際彙整

作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出（預
期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。

- 3 - 
個股報告 
Rubin平台升級推升 MLCC用量，國巨受惠供給排擠效應 
AI伺服器平台由 GB300進入 Rubin VR200後，單機架 MLCC用量提升 80%
與美元含量明顯提升 1.8 倍。量增主要來自 compute board 與 switch board，
新一代模組可望帶來的額外用量。更重要的是規格組合改變，高容值 MLCC
佔比提升後，將占用日韓廠較多高階產能資源。 
國巨並非高容值 AI MLCC的主要領導者 ， 這點反而使本輪受惠邏輯與日韓廠
不同。日韓廠直接承接高階AI MLCC需求，國巨則受惠其產能被排擠後，提
供一站式一般 IT、標準品與中高階 MLCC/被動元件解決方案。在消費性電子
衰退預期下，此優勢在同業中隨稼動率提升與交期拉長，國巨的議價環境轉
佳。 
與 2018 年不同，目前市場未見恐慌性囤貨的跡象。前次報告已提到，經銷
商 months on hand 已較一年前下降，且客戶以穩定供應為前提接受調價，
而非競相囤貨。這使本輪價格調整較接近供需正常化後的毛利修復，而不是
單純庫存循環。

鉭電容漲價趨勢延續，非 MLCC產品提高獲利穩定性 
鉭質電容仍為國巨本輪獲利上修的重要來源。AI伺服器功率密度提升，板面
空間受限，使鉭質電容在電源管理與高可靠度應用中的重要性提高。國巨透
過 KEMET 取得鉭電容領先地位，具備較高定價能力，且鉭電容訂單能見度
已延伸至下半年。 
研究部將鉭電容視為長期受需求成長、成本上升與供給結構影響的產品線。
原料成本上升有助於支撐價格調整，AI相關應用需求增加則帶動終端需求。
若後續價格調整順利反映成本變化，鉭電容獲利能力仍有進一步改善空間。 
除鉭電容外，電阻與電感亦為後續獲利支撐。晶片電阻用量與MLCC具高度
連動，國巨具備高市占與成本優勢；電感則受惠 AI伺服器 power module、
車用與電信應用需求。非MLCC產品線同步回升，可降低市場對單一MLCC
循環的疑慮。

獲利預估上修反映費用槓桿與營運效率改善 
前次報告已反映 26Q1 財報優於研究部原先模型、鉭電容漲價分批進入損益
表，以及通路庫存回到健康水位，但對費用槓桿估計偏保守。當時模型較接
近營收、毛利與費用同步上調的假設，因此低估營收上修後對營業利益率的
貢獻。 
本輪營收增量主要來自價格調整、稼動率提升與產品組合改善，並不需要等
比例增加銷售、管理與研發費用。換言之，若同樣增加 100 元營收，本輪有
較高比例可留在營業利益。研究部預估營業費用率由 26F 的 12.1%下降至
28F 的 9.4%，營業利益率由 27.1%提升至 32.4%。這是造成先前 EPS與目
標價低估的主因。

圖二、國巨(2327)各季營收及獲利走勢預估

資料來源：Cmoney；兆豐國際預估

獲利預估上修，費用槓桿支撐評價調升 
研究部預估國巨 2026 年營收 1,667.6 億元，YoY +25.4%，毛利率 39.2%，
稅後淨利 360.2 億元，EPS 17.4元；2027 年營收 2,004.0 億元，YoY 
+20.2%，毛利率 41.2%，稅後淨利 493.5 億元，EPS 23.8元；2028年營收
2,382.2 億元，YoY +18.9%，毛利率41.8%，稅後淨利605.0 億元，EPS 29.2
元。 
本次上修是重新評估 2026 至 2028 年價格調整、產能排擠與費用槓桿對損益
表的影響。毛利率提升來自價格調整、稼動率回升及高階品占比提升；營業
利益率提升則來自費用率下降。估值方面，評價基礎由 26F 移至 28F，反映
本輪被動元件循環並非單季或單年度事件，上調目標價 1,020元（35x 28(F) 
PER），潛在上漲空間 24.4%，投資評等維持「逢低買進」。

## Document 068: 260601_daiwa_Delta

Source PDF: all report pdf/260601_daiwa_Delta.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-06-01
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide an update on Delta after our 2026 Taiwan 
Corporate Day. We reaffirm our positive view on Delta and view it as one of 
our top ideas within the AI trend.

What’s the impact: Position in the power segment remains resilient. 
Based on our understanding, we believe there are four potential power 
architecture solutions to be applied in the Vera Rubin generation, with two 
from a high-voltage environment and the other two from a low-voltage 
environment. The high-voltage environment could be divided into 800VDC 
or +-400VDC, while the total voltage from rack could range from 600kW to 
1.5MW, depending on client requirements. As for low-voltage options, these 
can either be 72kW powershelf (bundled with 6x of 12kW PSUs) or 110kW 
powershelf (bundled with 6x of Nvidia’s standard 18.3kW solution), but 
width of 18.3kW power supply will be 3x bigger than Delta’s 12kW solution. 
We assume Nvidia’s strategy to use HVDC has sped up by c.1 year, and it 
would have 20-30% of HVDC adoption from this generation and 50% from 
2027. This is the key driver of our higher AI server revenue assumption.  
New growth drivers anticipated by investors. During Taiwan Corporate 
Day, we believe investor interest has gradually shifted to fuel cells (ie, 
SOFC or SOEC). Due to the technology licenses from Ceres Power (CWR 
L), Delta plans to build up trial fuel cell-capacity from 4Q26 and start mass 
production in 2027. We believe investors are keen to know the competition 
between Ceres and Bloom Energy in terms of tech spec and Delta’s 
competitors (Weichai [2338 HK, HKD40.14, Outperform (2); covered by 
Kelvin Lau] and Doosan Enerbility [034020 KS, KRW106,900, Buy (1); 
covered by Mike Oh]), which are also licensed from Ceres Power. We 
believe it will become the next growth driver for Delta after HVDC plays out. 
The micro grid (vs. utility grid) concept with rising new-type energy 
generation method (eg, fuel cells), solid-state transformers (SSTs), Energy 
Storage System (ESS) could enable Delta to further transform itself from a 
power systems company into an infrastructure provider.

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 8-9% to reflect 
better-than-expected gross margin performance and AI server contributions 
on rapid adoption of HVDC from Nvidia. We raise our 12M TP to TWD2,922 
(from TWD2,440), based on a higher target PER of 55x (from 50x; above 
its past-3-year range of 11-50x) applied to our 1-year-forward EPS, due to 
new growth drivers from the micro grid. We believe a higher target PER is 
justified as the PEG ratio stands at 0.90x (vs. our 2025-28E earnings 
CAGR of 61%). Downside risk: weaker-than-expected demand.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 4-36% above Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more bullish HVDC adoption rate.

1 June 2026

2026 Taiwan corporate day conference note

➢ Positive AI server power development with faster HVDC adoption  
➢ New growth drivers from fuel cell, SST, ESS and micro grid  
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating; lifting 12-month TP to TWD2,922

Company profile 
Founded in 1971, Delta Electronics has been the global leader in switching power supply solutions since 
2002 and DC brushless fans since 2006. Its business covers 3 key areas: power electronics (eg, power 
supplies, thermal management products and passive components), automation (eg, controllers, robot 
arms and building management systems), and infrastructure (eg, datacentre infrastructure, networking 
and EV charging).

## Document 069: 260601_daiwa_Fositek

Source PDF: all report pdf/260601_daiwa_Fositek.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-06-01
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Fositek joined our Daiwa Corporate Day on 29 May and 
shared an update on its business. We expect the company to benefit from 
the AI server trend in the coming years, with rising server revenue from 
QDs and slides over 2026-28E and share-gain potential in GPU and ASIC 
applications. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating. See our previous flash, Server 
segment should be the key earnings driver, 7 May 2026.

What’s the impact: Positive tone from the investor conference. For 
2Q26, Fositek guided revenue to be flat to up 5% QoQ, with double-digit  
QoQ revenue growth in both 3Q26 and 4Q26. We believe 2H26 revenue 
should be stronger than 1H26, supported by more ASIC projects, VR, LPX 
and Vera CPU projects. For CPO, Fositek is sampling QDs to CSP and 
GPU clients, which is likely to contribute revenue in 2027, according to 
management. Apart from AVC (see our latest flash on 14 May), Fositek is 
also testing with other thermal solution vendors, as well as new segments 
such as power rack. For slides, the company plans to tap into the liquid 
cooling segment in 2H26, with target revenue set to more than double YoY 
in 2026. For hinges for foldable phones, Fositek aims for flat YoY revenue 
in 2026, with the launch of three-fold phones in 3Q26 in a sole-supplier 
position. The company is also positive on the 2027 outlook, thanks to rising 
liquid cooling applications in VR and ASICs. It sees a high utilisation rate 
for QDs, with intact order visibility (without product transition concerns), and 
we believe there could be some upside potential to gross margin on 
improving yield rates and automation levels. On the gross margin side, 
Fositek expects to grow QoQ in 2026. We slightly cut our 2026E EPS by 
2% on more conservative revenue assumptions, but raise our 2027-28E 
EPS by 4-5% on our more aggressive revenue and gross margin 
assumptions. Note: Its product mix for 1Q26 is as below: foldable phone 
37.7% (-4.7% YoY), notebook 5.2% (+6.2% YoY), server 56.4% (+546.9% 
YoY) and others 0.7% (+8.4% YoY); its April 2026 product mix is: foldable 
phone 40.2%, notebook 5.7%, server 53.5% and others 0.6%

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call with an unchanged 
12M TP of TWD2,600, still based on a target PER of 33x (vs. its past-3-
year range of 9-43x [average of 23x]), translating to a 0.42x PEG over 
2025-27E on our 1-year forward EPS. Key downside risks: lower-than-
expected market share for key clients; slower-than-expected penetration 
rate of foldable phones; and geopolitical risks.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 1-7% above Bloomberg 
consensus, likely as we are more positive on its revenue and gross 
margins.

1 June 2026

Positive tone on the 2026-27 outlook

➢ Stronger revenue outlook in 2H26 vs. 1H26  
➢ The server segment should be the key revenue driver 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating with an unchanged 12M TP of TWD2,600

Company profile 
Fositek is a provider of foldable phone hinges, foldable laptop/tablet hinges, AR/VR glasses hinges, 
ultra-thin laptop hinges and keyboard hinges, as well as hinges for medical instruments and automobile 
applications. Its major clients include Huawei and Motorola.

Powerchip Semiconductor Manufacturing Co  | Asia
Pacific
Profitability turnaround in April
Turning profitable in April: Powerchip reported Apr 2026 net profit of NT$552mn,
+243.46% Y/Y; April 2026 EPS of NT$0.13, +244.44% Y/Y. Apr 2026 net margin was
10.94%. This is not comparable to 1Q26 net margin, which was skewed by NT
$20.1bn gains from the Fab P5 disposal and NT$5.5bn related impairments booked in
1Q. Excluding these gains and impairments, 1Q26 net margin would be -2.7%.

Therefore,  April's profit reflects the company actually turning profitable excluding
one-off items. The 10.94% margin is also stronger than our 2Q26e net margin
estimate of 7.1%. April EPS of NT$0.13 represents 66% of our 2Q26e EPS estimate
of NT$0.29.
Reiterate OW
:  we believe Powerchip is a beneficiary of mature node up-cycle and
EMIB supply chain. The company's improved profitability reflects its ability to
benefit from the mature mode up-cycle, and the volume ramp-up of the company's

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Powerchip Semiconductor Manufacturing Co (6770.TW)
Base case, 2.1x 2026e P/B. This method is in line with our approach for Greater China memory
IDM players, in view of the industry's high volatility. Our 2.1x target P/B is higher than the
historical average of 1.5x since 2021, reflecting our optimistic view on improved pricing and
utilization.
Risks to Upside
n Pricing power is sustained in 2026.

n Listing on main board attracts investment fund flows.
n Aluminum process enjoys higher margins.
Risks to Downside
n Competition from China intensifies.
n Price erosion is faster than expected.
n Demand from consumer electronics clients weakens further.
n Inventory in the supply chain takes longer to digest.

Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C.
Auras (3324.TWO): Computex 2026: Liquid cooling total solution with
in-row CDU showcased; Buy
2 June 2026 | 11:26PM HKT
We visited Auras’ booth on June 2 at our Computex tour in Taipei. Auras
showcased its liquid cooling and air cooling solution, covering components across
CDU (Cooling Distribution Unit), UQD (Universal Quick Disconnect), cold plate,
manifold etc. Management highlighted the rising adoption of liquid cooling solution,

driven by the AI infrastructure requiring higher efficienc y and lower power
consumption. The company has launched in-row CDU to work under 3 degree ATC
(Approach Temperature Differ ence) environment, and a water pump product to
support PWM (Pulse Width Modulation) demand. We expect to see triple digit YoY
earnings growth in 2026E, with rising contribution from liquid cooling and higher
blended GM. Maintain Buy.
Key takeaways
1. Rising liquid cooling penetration: Overall, management is positive on the rising

adoption of liquid cooling solutions to lower the power consumption of AI
infrastructure and the total operational cost. The company has expanded from GPU
/ CPU cooling solution to ASIC, and the company’s CPU / DIMM (Dual In-line Memory
Module) dual cooling module is featured with high accuracy and reliability,
supporting rising CPU workload and AI data management.
2. Cooling products in expansion: The company showcased its liquid cooling total
solutions for AI servers across various architectures. Management highlighted the

in-row CSU could support up to 2MW cooling efficiency under 3 degree ATC
(Approach Temperature Difference) environment. Besides offering UQD and MQD
(Multiple Quick Disconnect), mgmt noted Auras also develops the water pump
products for PWM (Pulse Width Modulation) to balance the performance and power
consumption, which is used across AI data center, work station and Notebook.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Auras
Our 12m target price for Auras is based on a near-term P/E, consistent with our Taiwan
Technology coverage. We base our 12-m TP of NT$1,283 on a target P/E multiple of
17.9x forward year EPS (2027E). Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between
P/E and EPS growth of its peers in technology hardware, based on the company’s
2027E-28E EPS YoY growth at 23%. Key downside risks: 1) Slower-than-expected liquid

cooling penetration; (2) Soft AI server demand; (3) Weaker-than-expected PC market;
and (4) Potential competition from existing and new suppliers.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

Elite Material (2383.TW): Taiwan Computex & Corporate Day: Capacity
expansion to capture doubled demand from major ASIC project in 2027
3 June 2026 | 10:50AM CST
We hosted meetings with EMC at our Taiwan Computex & Corporate Day (June
2nd). Key takeaways include:
(1) For a specific CSP cus tomer, EMC is expected to gain market share in 2027 as
demand will double while Japanese competitors are unable to keep up according to

the company, leading to incremental share gains. In terms of its 3 largest AI
customers’ CCL supply chain, the company continues to target 50%+ market share in
the long term.
(2) The company expects target capacity to reach 9.30mn sheets/month by 2027
end (slightly below the previous 9.45mn sheets/month target due to allocation of
space for office construction). Despite the minor adjustment, management believes
the company is well positioned to capture additional market share through its

proactive expansion plan, while Japanese and Korean peers continue to expand at a
much slower pace.
(3) The company is entering the high-end AI substrate CCL industry with the new
300k sheets/month capacity in Taiwan released in 4Q26 dedicated to this new
business (the company’s AI substrate CCL capacity could reach ~1mn sheets/month
if fully converted). The company is entering this market to capture market share
amid supply shortage, with a focus on serving customers in Taiwan and mainland

China.
(4) The MP (mass production) for M9 CCL will start from 2H26, and management
expects Nvidia to be the first to adopt M9 CCL, with ASIC customers to follow suit.
The highest end CCL that most ASIC projects currently utilize is M8+ grade, with a
high probability of migrating to M9 grade in 2027. EMC believes the M9 CCL will
contribute at least low single digits % of total revenue in 2026 and could move up to
double digits % of total revenue in 2027.

(5) The company continues to make progress with M8.5/M9 CCL, which is
differentiated by glass fiber type (low-DK for M8.5 and Q-glass for M9), and
indicated that both solutions remain viable at this stage, while final adoption is still
dependent on Nvidia, and backplane is still one year away from production, thus
design is not finalized yet.
(6) For high-end product (M7 and above grade CCL) revenue contribution, the
company believes the number should go up from 50%+ in 2025 to at least 60% in

### Page 2

Investment view
We maintain our positive view on EMC considering the market share gain opportunities
in ASIC driven by a major CSP client doubling the demand in 2027, while a key Japanese
competitor has no capacity expansion plan and is unable to meet the incremental
demand. The company’s proactive capacity expansion plans (26% 2025-27E CAGR
capacity expansion vs. industry average at 15-20% 2025-27E CAGR) should continue to
lead to high market share in the high-end CCL industry. Moreover, considering the

company’s leadership position in technology, we expect the company should be the
major supplier for M9 grade CCL in the next 12-24months, with ASP for M9 grade CCL at
over 5x vs. EMC’s current product average ASP , suggesting solid revenue upside potential
for EMC in coming quarters/years.
EMC, a key supplier of high-end HDI material (70%+ market share) and SLP material
(90%+ market share), has been focusing on the high-speed switch/server CCL market for

3-5+ years. We are positive on its market share trajectory in the server industry and
expect EMC to increase its share from <10% in the Purley generation to 15-20%/20+%
in the Whitley/Eagle Stream platforms. EMC is also proactively expanding its share in the
switch market (40%/30%+ in 2024/23 vs. <5% before 2019), with an expanding AI
customer base (only one 400G customer in 3Q20, to being one of the largest suppliers in
2023), which should continue to benefit the company’s top/bottom line growth over

time. We believe EMC’s leading position in the AI server CCL market should drive strong
revenue growth and GM/OPM expansion in the long term, given that it is the major
supplier for all Nvidia/Google/AWS AI server projects, and we expect it to maintain its
leadership position. Considering its leading position in the high-end CCL industry, solid
production skills and earnings growth outlook, we are Buy rated on the stock. With the
shares trading at a 2026E P/E below AI server component suppliers and supported by a

solid growth outlook, we view valuation as attractive.
Key downside risks include: (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2)
rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and
(3) rising competition from mainland China peers.
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$6,000 is based on 27x 2027E P/E (in line
with the peak P/E multiple for AI component suppliers in the past 3 years).
Key downside risks:  (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) rising

trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and (3)
rising competition from mainland China peers.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at
https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Greater China Technology Semiconductors

Industry View Attractive
Old Memory: Readacross from
Kioxia IR Day
Legacy NAND technology powers high performance SSD: On its IR Day (link),

Kioxia (covered by Kazuo Yoshikawa) highlighted its GP series high-performance
SSD, which features 100m+ IOPS, ultra-fast and low-latency performance to
accelerate next-gen AI. The company said it is compatible with Nvidia’s Storage-Next
solution. The GP series SSD is used to expand GPU memory to resolve system
memory constraints, and enables large-scale DB
(database) and faster generation.
The GP series SSD (XL-Flash) has adopted Kioxia's proprietary SLC chip.

Implications for Greater China memory stocks: We mentioned in our note, Old
Memory: Upside Surprise Ahead, that legacy NAND technology could be ideal for
fast reading and writing speeds and hence could see expanded TAM from adoption
in datacenters. We view the confirmation from Kioxia as positive for GigaDevice,
Winbond, and Macronix, which are legacy NAND vendors in our coverage and remain
OW on all three stocks.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Macronix International Co Ltd (2337.TW)
Base case, P/B, in line with our Greater China memory coverage, due to the memory industry's
cyclical nature. We apply a target multiple of 7.0x to our 2026 BVPS estimate, vs. its historical
average of 1.5x since 2017. We continue to believe the P/B target is justifiable, as The MLC and
legacy TLC NAND could be in greater shortage into 2H26, with under supply easily going up

to 40%. The only supplier which could fill the gap remains Macronix.
Risks to Upside
n Wider legacy NAND supply / demand gap
n NOR up-cycle, driven by limited supply expansion and/or stronger demand
n Faster growth for Switch hardware and core titles
Risks to Downside
n Less severe legacy NAND supply / demand gap
n NOR down-cycle, driven by faster supply expansion and/or demand weakness
n Slower growth for Switch hardware and core titles
Winbond Electronics Corp (2344.TW)

Base case, 5.5x 2026e P/B. This method is in line with the approach we adopt for Greater China
memory IDM peers, in view of the industry's high volatility. We believe the stock merits a mul-
tiple beyond the high end of its historical band of 0.5-1.5x since 2017, as we are seeing DRAM
pricing upside, a sustainable NOR price hike, and opportunities in SLC NAND and SiCap
foundry business.
Risks to Upside
n NOR Flash pricing increase, driven by stronger-than-expected demand

n Stronger-than-expected DRAM pricing given ongoing supply shortage
n Faster-than-expected SLC NAND development
Risks to Downside
n NOR Flash downcycle, driven by weaker demand
n Lower-than-expected DRAM pricing given ongoing oversupply
n Slower-than-expected SLC NAND development
GigaDevice Semiconductor Beijing Inc (603986.SS)
Our price target is our base case value, derived from our residual income model. Key assump-
tions: cost of equity of 8.9% (beta 1.1, risk-free rate 2% and risk premium 6%), a payout ratio

of 40%, medium-term growth rate of 16.4%, and a terminal growth rate of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n NOR up-cycle driven by stronger demand
n Superior chip design, leading to increasing exposure to low-density NOR
n Faster-than-expected DRAM development

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 104: 260604_daiwa_Taiwan Datacentre Hardware

Source PDF: all report pdf/260604_daiwa_Taiwan Datacentre Hardware.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-06-04

### Page 1

Summary: During Computex 2026, we arranged booth tours for our investors with 
representatives of companies within our coverage, including Wiwynn, Aspeed, Delta, Hon 
Hai, and Chenbro, alongside several other companies. In conjunction with keynote 
addresses delivered by leading global technology companies, we believe the primary focus 
of Computex 2026 remained centered on the CPU domain - specifically, the role of CPUs 
in orchestrating the broader ecosystem for agentic AI, as well as the emerging total 
addressable market (TAM) arising from AI PCs incorporating NVIDIA's Spark chipset. 
Across the booths we visited, the majority of vendors showcased components and 
solutions applicable to NVIDIA's Vera Rubin generation platform. These demonstrations 
should be regarded as indicative of trial production activity within the Taiwan supply chain 
ahead of the official volume ramp, which is anticipated to commence in 4Q26, with the 
underlying concepts having been primarily introduced at GTC. With respect to key 
technology transitions, the developments that garnered the most attention at the 
conference included CPO-based AI server architecture, Co-Packaging Connectors (CPC), 
High-Voltage Direct Current (HVDC) power delivery, and microgrid solutions. In conclusion, 
we encourage investors to maintain a sustained focus on CPU-related investment themes, 
as well as on specification upgrades driven primarily by escalating high-speed 
transmission requirements. Our key Buy ideas include: Aspeed, Lotes (3533 TT, 
TWD2,400), Wiwynn, Delta, TUC (6274 TT, TWD1,695), EMC (2383 TT, TWD4,830), and 
Accton (2345 TT, TWD2,510).

Wiwynn (6669 TT, TWD5,570, Buy [1]) 
• According to investor feedback, Wiwynn's booth was widely regarded as one of the 
most compelling exhibitions at Computex, owing to its demonstration of a Co-Packaged 
Optics (CPO) computing rack. The demonstration was developed in collaboration with 
several key partners, including Ayar Labs - contributing the optical engine and External 
Laser Small Form Factor Pluggable (ELSFP) technology - and GUC (3443 TT 
TWD4,400, Hold [3], covered by Rick Hsu), contributing the XPU. It is our understanding 
that the demonstration remains under development, and the collaboration with Ayar 
Labs is currently in a pilot stage. Wiwynn's objective is to ensure that it acquires the 
requisite capabilities and expertise ahead of CPO technology reaching broader 
commercial maturity. Based on our observations, Wiwynn's focus appears to be 
concentrated on computing tray development, while the scale-out and scale-up switch 
racks are expected to be contributed by other partners. 
• Another notable highlight from Wiwynn's exhibition was its demonstration of AMD's 
Helios platform. Based on our understanding, AMD's Helios architecture would comprise 
18 computing units, each configured with four GPUs and one CPU per rack. Given the 
physical dimensions of AMD's MI450 series GPU and connectivity speed requirements, 
the GPU layout is positioned at the bottom of the PCB in order to optimize connectivity 
with the scale-up system. According to Wiwynn, the Helios platform will leverage UALink 
for scale-up interconnects, while adopting Ethernet for scale-out connectivity 
(implementing UALink over Ethernet).

Aspeed (5274 TT, TWD18,265, Buy [1]) 
• At Computex 2026, Aspeed's showcase highlighted its evolution from a pure Baseboard 
Management Controller (BMC) supplier toward a broader platform management and

infrastructure control provider. The company demonstrated solutions spanning BMC, 
Satellite Management Controller (SMC, or mini-BMC), I/O expansion, and security IC. 
• A key emphasis at Aspeed's booth was the company's adoption of RISC-V architecture 
and OCP open-source frameworks to support Caliptra — an IP designed to execute 
root-of-trust functions. This security enhancement is expected to be implemented across 
the majority of Aspeed's product lines, thereby elevating the overall security standard of 
its devices. 
• Another significant highlight was the introduction of the AST1840, succeeding the 
previous AST1800. The AST1700/1800/1840 series constitutes Aspeed's I/O expander 
product line, with the AST1800 marking the first edition to incorporate an embedded 
FPGA within the I/O expander. With the AST1840, Aspeed has transitioned to 
outsourcing the FPGA chip from Lattice Semiconductor, resulting in the development of 
a new System-on-Chip (SoC) for the AST1840. 
• Regarding the near-term outlook, following the company's revenue guidance of 
TWD4.1-4.3 billion, we anticipate an additional revenue step-up from 4Q26, as Aspeed 
has qualified with a second OSAT partner, enabling broader support from substrate 
vendors. With respect to the T-glass to E-glass substrate certification, we expect the 
transition to be completed successfully; however, the initial production batch of the 
AST2700 will incorporate substrates containing both T-glass and E-glass. Over the 
longer term, only the E-glass substrate version is expected to remain in production. 
• The company expressed a continued positive outlook on CPU server demand, driven by 
the growth of agentic AI workloads. According to Aspeed's latest forecast, BMC unit 
shipments are projected to reach approximately 8.5 million units in 2026. Looking ahead 
to 2027, we think BMC shipments from the conventional server segment will grow by at 
least 30% year-over-year.

Delta Electronics (2308 TT, TWD2,425, Buy [1]) 
• Delta’s Computex 2026 showcase its strategy of extending from power and thermal 
components into broader AI datacentre infrastructure. Delta highlighted five main 
exhibition areas: modular datacentre, micro grid solutions, HVDC solutions, Vera Rubin 
solutions, and Edge AI. We believe the key investment implication is that Delta is 
positioning itself not only as a supplier of power supplies and cooling components, but 
also as an infrastructure partner spanning power conversion. 
• 800VDC remains the core product direction. Delta’s key AI datacentre message is built 
around 800VDC architecture. Its showcase included an 800VDC in-row power system, 
90kW DC-DC power shelf, 110kW AC-DC power shelf, 12kW power distribution board, 
and DC-DC conversion modules. The 800VDC in-row power system is designed to 
simplify the power conversion architecture and achieve over 98% system efficiency, 
while also integrating 480kW of total BBU for short-duration support to critical loads. We 
see this as the next step in Delta’s power roadmap as AI rack power emerge. 
• Micro-grid is becoming the next key question from investors on top of HVDC, while SST 
and SOFC becomes the key discussion. However, these new growth drivers likely won’t 
bear fruit in just 1-2 years. The microgrid solution integrates renewables, energy 
storage, fuel cells and other power sources to support datacentre operation in grid-
connected and off-grid modes. Delta’s solid-state transformer (SST) can directly convert 
medium-voltage AC to low-voltage 800VDC, with high power density and modular 
design to reduce footprint and power losses. The SOFC fuel cell offers 60% generation 
efficiency and 24-hour stable power supply with modular expansion. We see SST and 
SOFC as strategic long-term options tied to AI datacentre power trend, while the nearer-
term investment focus remains 800VDC power racks, power shelves, distribution 
boards, BBU, and liquid cooling.

### Page 3

Taiwan Datacentre Hardware 
4 June 2026 
3

Hon Hai (2317 TT, TWD293.0, Buy [1]) 
• As one of NVIDIA's key strategic partners, Hon Hai presented its comprehensive lineup 
of NVIDIA AI servers at Computex. Notably, the Vera CPU rack and CPO switch were 
absent from the exhibition. Hon Hai's demonstration included new computing trays and 
switches for the VR200, as well as the HGX NVL8 — a new HGX configuration featuring 
eight GPUs, dual x86 CPUs, and integrated liquid-cooling components. The compact 
design of the NVL8 enables a reduced form factor of 2U (with a 1U height of 45mm). 
• In addition, Hon Hai showcased its Co-Packaging Connector (CPC), designed for 
integration into switches such as the Spectrum-X. Based on our understanding, Hon 
Hai/FIT is capable of delivering 224 Gbps per lane, achieving an aggregate throughput 
of 12.8 Tbps per connector. We believe that at least two clients are currently evaluating 
the adoption of this CPC technology.

• From FIT's perspective, the company also presented its Paladin connector, intended for 
use within the mid-plane of the computing tray. This connector, along with the mid-plane, 
is expected to serve as a key replacement for Amphenol's flyover cable. We believe FIT 
is actively developing a next-generation connector solution to be deployed within the 
Kyber architecture, where the mid-plane will serve as the interconnect between both the 
computing and switch trays.

Chenbro (8210 TT, TWD1,420, Buy [1]) 
• As an established rack vendor on NVIDIA's Reference Vendor List (RVL), Chenbro 
showcased several rack solutions compatible with NVIDIA's VR NVL72 at the exhibition. 
• We understand that Chenbro is currently engaged in multiple new rack projects, 
encompassing noise-cancellation chassis, IT racks for NVIDIA/AMD, liquid-cooling racks 
(including CDU racks in both Liquid-to-Liquid and Liquid-to-Air configurations), and 
switch racks. This strategic expansion underscores Chenbro's transition from a chassis 
vendor to a full-scale rack vendor, reflecting the broader industry shift whereby end 
customers are increasingly designing their infrastructure at the rack level rather than the 
chassis level. 
• We anticipate that the margin profile for rack solutions will reach c.25-30%, compared to 
our previous estimate of c.15-20%, driven by the significant degree of customization 
involved. Consequently, our revised long-term gross margin forecast has been lifted 
from 25-26% to 28-29%.  
• During the booth tour, Chenbro emphasized its enhanced development cycle, which is 
designed to further reduce time-to-market for its clients. The company also highlighted 
its diversified development teams - comprising software engineers, industrial designers, 
mechanical engineers, and other specialists - to provide comprehensive support to end 
customers. We believe Chenbro's role is a key co-design and the most fast partner for 
its end customers, making it a critical differentiator from its competitors and mitigating 
the impact of competitive pressures.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at
https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

### Page 1

M
Update
Advantech  | Asia Pacific
May Sales -6% MoM/+32% YoY
In this report, we focus on Advantech's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• May sales were NT$7,734mn (-6% MoM/+32% YoY).
• QTD revenue reached 75% of consensus at NT$21,274mn (+4% QoQ/+19%
YoY) and 77% of the company's guidance at NT$20,790mn (+2% QoQ/+17%
YoY at mid-point).
Our view:
• We see 2Q26 revenue tracking ahead of market expectations.

• Management sees limited GM impact from elevated component costs, as it
remains confident in passing most incremental costs to customers  (
see our
report).
• A solid B/B ratio continues to point to a strong revenue outlook, supported
by demand from the semi equipment, robotics, and smart manufacturing
segments.
• We remain constructive on Advantech, as it is well positioned to benefit
from accelerating IoT market growth amid edge AI proliferation.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Advantech (2395.TW)
Base case, 30x 2025e P/E. We view our target multiple as justified by our 19% earnings CAGR
forecast for 2024-26, as it implies a PEG of 1.6, vs. ~3 over 2014-21. It has also been tempered
too reflect tariff uncertainty.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected macroeconomic conditions
n More top- and bottom-line growth from the platform and applications
Risks to Downside

n Weaker macro environment
n Slower progress in WISE-PaaS and solution-ready packages

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

M
Update
Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
May  Sales -6% MoM/+133% YoY
In this report, we focus on Chroma's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for share price movement.
Details:
• May revenue was NT$4,550mn (-6% MoM/+133% YoY).
• QTD revenue reached 75% of MSe at NT$12,575mn (+6% QoQ/+95% YoY)
and 76% of consensus of NT$12,359mn (+4% QoQ/+91% YoY).
• Parent company revenue was NT$3,744mn (-5% QoQ/+135% YoY).
Our view:
• We see 2Q26 tracking ahead of market expectations.

• We expect Chroma to see strong revenue growth in 2026, driven the power
testing (AI power product capacity expansions, emerging HVDC migration,
and ESS upturn) and semi & photonics (new SL T capex cycle, metrology for
advanced packaging and photonics testing for transceivers/CPO) businesses
amid AI deployment.
• Moreover, FT handlers and burn-in ovens are under qualification, with
revenue contribution expected in the coming years.
• Stay OW.

### Page 3

M
Update
Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
Base case, 50x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it better
captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average valuation of
~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating profit CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG of 1.0. We think
our target multiple of 50x is justified by the 53% operating profit CAGR we project for

2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected macro conditions
n Stronger semi and photonics and power testing demand
n Margin improvement via better product mix
n Better opex control
Risks to Downside
n Macro slowdown
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower progress in the advanced packaging capacity expansion
n Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand
n Margin contraction arising from lack of revenue scale and increasing opex

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

08 Jun 2026 14:04:50 ET │ 14 pages
Gold Circuit Electronics (2368.TW)
Record monthly sales backed by robust demand; Buy
CITI'S TAKE
Gold Circuit saw strong April–May sales driven by a better product mix (i.e.,
networking), higher ASP , and likely some new capacity ramp, in our view. We
expect stronger sales momentum in the coming months given the start of
production ramp for its existing ASIC customer. Given its market share gain
in 2026, we expect GCE’s plants to be fully utilized in the coming quarters.

Further, the company sees a high likelihood of supplying a new ASIC
customer, which we view as an important growth driver for 2027. We believe
GCE’s aggressive capacity expansion will help it capture more AI demand.
Reiterate Buy with higher TP of NT$1,740 (26x 2027E EPS).
Strong sales uptick— GCE reported NT$8.8bn sales in May (+88% YoY, +20% MoM),
driven in our view by ASP hike, enhanced product mix, and some sales delayed from
April. Combined April and May sales account for c.70% of our 2Q26 Citi/consensus

forecasts. Going forward, we believe the company could fully reflect the CCL price
hike on end-customers given its leading PCB player position and tight capacity.
ASIC demand ramping up; potential new customer from 4Q26 — We expect
existing ASIC demand to start ramping up in late 2Q26, with more meaningful
contribution from 3Q26. GCE will likely leverage its Thailand plant to support the
ASIC demand. We expect the company to gain market share at its existing ASIC

customer. Management also sees a high likelihood of securing a new AI ASIC
customer starting 4Q26. The company could supply its AI ASIC mainboard with
> 30L layer count design.
Continued capacity ramp to catch up with demand — This year, GCE is taking
measures to debottleneck and ramp up additional capacity in Taiwan and Thailand.
We also see it aiming to ramp up with one greenfield plant each year in 2027 /28/29E
across Thailand, China, and Taiwan. Despite the three plants currently planned,

management thinks overall capacity is still not able to fulfill customer demand.
Earnings/TP upward revisions; higher TP NT$1,7 40 — We raise our 2026/27 /28E
earnings by 4%/4%/6% to reflect strong sales and enhanced product mix. We raise
our TP to NT$1,740 (26x 2027E EPS) from NT$1,650 (26x 2027E EPS). We believe the
high PE multiple of 26x is justified by GCE’s market share gain with its existing ASIC
customer and potential to secure a new ASIC customer. Reiterate Buy.

Buy

Bull/Bear: Gold Circuit Electronics (2368.TW)

### Page 6

Gold Circuit Electronics (2368.TW)
09 June 2026 Citi Research
6
Gold Circuit Electronics
Company description
Founded in 1981, Gold Circuit Electronics (GCE) is a Taiwanese company
specializing in the production of printed circuit board (PCB) including double-
sided PCB, multi-layer PCB, and HDI. GCE’s products primarily focus on
applications of servers, networking, laptops, base stations, mobile phones,
and tablets. The company is known for its high-layer count design

capabilities (maximum layer count: +50L) in the PCB industry, which help it to
penetrate into more high-end applications such as AI servers and 400G
networking. Currently, server/networking accounts for +70% of its total sales.
The company has four plants, with one located in Taiwan and the other three
in China, and plans to build one plant in Thailand.
Investment strategy
We rate GCE a Buy as we believe its high-tech capabilities in
server/networking design will allow it to catch more AI opportunities from US

CSPs or US IC designers in the AI boom cycle. We expect its rising sales
exposure toward high-end applications with better margins and improving
UTR to drive its margin going forward. In our view, geopolitical tensions
should benefit non-China suppliers more, which would prevent GCE from
fierce ASP competition from China PCB peers.
Valuation
Our TP of NT$1,740 for GCE is based on 26x our 2027E EPS estimate, which is
higher than 22x or +2std level of its 1-year-forward PE over the past three

years. We believe the 26x multiple is justified by GCE's high AI
server/networking sales exposure, market share gain in existing ASIC
customer and new AI ASIC customer wins from 4Q26, which drives its AI ASIC
sales growth and margin profile. Recall that TSMC expects close to a 50%+
CAGR in the next five years for its AI-related demand, which is stronger than
any other applications. Thus, we believe investors would be willing to pay for
high-growth AI names (especially ASIC) with high PE multiples. Our target

price is equivalent to 44x/26x our 2026/27E EPS estimates and 16.7x/10.2x
our 2026/27E BVPS.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected server recovery; 2) slower ramp-up
pace of new server platform; 3) less-than-expected AI demand; 4) more PCB
peers added into AI supply chain; and 5) stricter practice of China+1 by
customers due to geopolitical concerns.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.

Gold Circuit Electronics (2368.TW)

9
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

Chenbro  | Asia Pacific
May Sales +22% MoM/+37%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Chenbro's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• May sales were NT$2,517mn (+22% MoM/+37% YoY).
• QTD sales reached 60% of our 2Q26 estimate at NT$7,596mn (+7% QoQ/
+40% YoY) and 59% of consensus at NT$7,736mn (+9% QoQ/+42% YoY).
Our view:
• 2Q26 revenue is tracking largely in line with our expectations.
• Management remains optimistic on the 2H26 outlook, supported by GPU/

ASIC and general purpose servers, as well as increasing rack shipments.
• Chenbro is also tapping into more rack-level mechanical components and
integration services, by leveraging its expertise in the chassis business.
• We maintain the view that Chenbro will benefit from AI server shipment
growth and its share gains, as well as the incremental contribution from rack
offerings in the coming years.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chenbro (8210.TW)
Base case, derived from 30x 2027e P/E. We view our target multiple as justified by the 33%
earnings CAGR we see for 2025-28, which implies a PEG of 0.9. This is in line the average of
0.9 over 2014-24, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 12x for a 14% earnings
CAGR.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger AI capex outlook
n Larger market share gains within existing customers

n Faster penetration into new customers
n Higher entry barrier among peers that leads to higher ASP/profitability
n Better macro outlook
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Less share gains within existing customers
n Slower penetration into new customers
n Intensifying competition leading to lower ASP/profitability
n Continuous macro uncertainties
